}
const OPTIONAL:&str="table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo,owner:viewer}\n";
#[test]fn optional_media_is_not_required_by_a_name_but_exact_nonnull_intent_is(){
 assert!(!has(OPTIONAL,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 for predicate in [".photo != null","not (.photo = null)",".photo != null and .owner = viewer"]{assert!(has(&format!("{OPTIONAL}invariant docs always {predicate}\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"{predicate}");}
 for predicate in [".photo != null or .owner = viewer","not (.photo != null)",".owner != null"]{assert!(!has(&format!("{OPTIONAL}invariant docs always {predicate}\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"{predicate}");}
 assert!(!has(&OPTIONAL.replace("photo: image <=10000 ?):","photo: image <=10000):"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}
const CHILD:&str="table parents:\n  n: int default 0\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable photos:\n  parent: parents\n  photo: image <=10000\n  unique parent\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?):\n  p = insert parents {}\n  if photo != null:\n    insert photos {parent:p.id,photo}\n  return p\n";
#[test]fn required_child_columns_do_not_make_an_optional_attachment_mandatory(){
 assert!(!has(CHILD,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));assert!(has(CHILD,"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE"));
 let required=format!("{CHILD}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n");assert!(has(&required,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let guarded=required.replace("  p = insert parents {}","  require photo != null else REQUIRED\n  p = insert parents {}");assert!(!has(&guarded,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}
#[test]fn asserting_an_optional_photo_enforces_required_creation_without_a_review_gate(){
 for s in [include_str!("security/wave23/wave18-046.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/wave19-046.q")]{assert!(!has(s,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));}
 let asserted=OPTIONAL.replace("{photo,owner:viewer}","{photo:photo ! else REQUIRED,owner:viewer}");assert!(!has(&format!("{asserted}invariant docs always .photo != null\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let unconditional=CHILD.replace("  if photo != null:\n    insert photos {parent:p.id,photo}","  insert photos {parent:p.id,photo:photo ! else REQUIRED}");assert!(!has(&format!("{unconditional}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let conditional=CHILD.replace("add(photo: image <=10000 ?)","add(photo: image <=10000 ?, enabled:bool)").replace("if photo != null:","if enabled:").replace("parent:p.id,photo}","parent:p.id,photo:photo ! else REQUIRED}");assert!(!has(&format!("{conditional}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"independent boolean skips remain parent integrity/test coverage, not an upload-omission gate");
 let omitted=CHILD.replace("parent:p.id,photo}","parent:p.id,photo:photo ! else REQUIRED}");assert!(has(&format!("{omitted}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let helper=OPTIONAL.replace("{photo,owner:viewer}","{photo:checked(photo),owner:viewer}")+"fn checked(photo:image <=10000 ?):\n  return photo ! else REQUIRED\n";assert!(!has(&format!("{helper}invariant docs always .photo != null\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let nullable=OPTIONAL.replace("add(photo: image <=10000 ?)","add(photo: image <=10000 ?, enabled:bool)").replace("{photo,owner:viewer}","{photo:(photo ! else REQUIRED) if enabled else null,owner:viewer}");assert!(!has(&format!("{nullable}invariant docs always .photo != null\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"nullable alternatives remain an integrity/test question, not a proven argument-omission gate");
}

#[test]fn changed_field_guards_survive_a_helper_and_lexical_aliases(){
 let s="table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret: text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\nfn editHelper(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  next = secret\n  if next != old.secret:\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update docs[doc] {secret:next}\n  return null\nmutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return editHelper(doc,secret)\n";
 assert!(has(s,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let equality=s.replace("if next != old.secret:\n    require", "if next = old.secret:\n    require true else SAME\n  else:\n    require");assert!(has(&equality,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let reassigned=s.replace("    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update", "    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  else:\n    next = 'changed'\n  update");assert!(!has(&reassigned,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let multiple=s.replace("  if next != old.secret:", "  update docs[doc] {secret:'intervening'}\n  if next != old.secret:");assert!(!has(&multiple,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}
#[test]fn effectful_loop_domains_never_reuse_a_preloop_column_guard(){

 let rejected=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())]);assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.code=="E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT"),"the original helper-list counterexample is already outside the language");

codex
The upload gate has the two stated alternatives, including required parent presence with nullable child media. The focused tests cover separate sites and columns, actual-index identity, and generated-table regressions. I’m now checking whether suppression and guard recovery preserve those boundaries.

//! Public check gates are individual dispositions, independent of proof verdicts.
use std::{fs,path::PathBuf,process::Command};
use serde_json::Value;
struct Scratch(PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn scratch()->Scratch{let p=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-cli-{}-{}",std::process::id(),{static NEXT:std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize=std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);NEXT.fetch_add(1,std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)}));fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();Scratch(p)}

#[test]fn one_note_at_a_time_bulk_cannot_clear_and_source_edits_invalidate(){
 let p=scratch();let source=p.0.join("snapback/schema.q");fs::write(&source,SOURCE).unwrap();

 assert_eq!(j["securityReviews"],check(&p,&[]).1["securityReviews"],"same input must produce stable IDs");

 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-notes"]).0,1);assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","reviewed unreachable draft creation helper"]).0,1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[1],"--reason","reviewed unreachable second draft creation helper"]).0,0);

 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","  "]).0,2);assert_eq!(fs::read(&review).unwrap(),bytes);

 let (code,j)=check(&p,&["--ack-security",&ids[0],"--reason","reviewed first unreachable draft insertion"]);assert_eq!(code,1);assert_eq!(j["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|d|d["gated"]==true&&d["acknowledged"]==false).count(),1);

}
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/authority_walk.rs
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/columns.rs
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/authority_walk/changes.rs
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
mod changes;
use changes::Change;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],changes:&[Change],column:Option<&str>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    let changes=if column.is_some()&&changes::prior_effects(&expr){&[][..]}else{changes};
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();if let Some(column)=column{for change in changes{if change.matches(e,column,p){context.body.extend(change.requirements.clone());}}}out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,changes,column,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,&mut changes.to_vec(),column,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,changes:&mut Vec<Change>,column:Option<&str>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{
        let consuming=column.is_some()&&changes::effectful(s);
        match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::Emit{payload,once,..}=>{
            if column.is_some(){changes.clear();}
            expression(catalog,p,payload,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            if let Some(once)=once{expression(catalog,p,once,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);}
        },
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,site}=>{
            if column.is_some()&&changes::expression_effectful(&resolve(condition,env)){changes.clear();}
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            let condition=resolve(condition,env);
            let mut then_guards=guards.clone();
            then_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:condition.clone(),code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut then_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
            let mut else_guards=guards.clone();
            else_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:Expr::Unary{op:"not".into(),value:Box::new(condition.clone()),site:site.clone()},code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut else_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
            let assigned=branch_assignments(then_body).into_iter().chain(branch_assignments(else_body)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if column.is_some()&&assigned.is_empty(){changes.extend(changes::after(catalog,&condition,then_body,else_body,env));}
            for name in assigned{env.insert(name.clone(),Expr::Name{name:format!("$advice_unknown_{name}"),site:site.clone()});}
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,site}=>{if column.is_some(){changes.clear();}expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),&mut Vec::new(),column,depth,out);for name in branch_assignments(body){env.insert(name.clone(),Expr::Name{name:format!("$advice_unknown_{name}"),site:site.clone()});}},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out),_=>{}
    }
    if consuming{changes.clear();}
    }
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,None)}
pub(super) fn column_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:&str)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,Some(column))}
fn walk_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:Option<&str>)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();let mut changes=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);out
}

fn branch_assignments(body:&[Stmt])->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();for s in body{match s{Stmt::Bind{names,..}=>out.extend(names.clone()),Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.extend(branch_assignments(then_body));out.extend(branch_assignments(else_body));},Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>out.extend(branch_assignments(body)),_=>{}}}out
}

//! Changed-column guards are advisory and bind the exact row, field and new value.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — no table-wide promotion of a column condition.
use super::*;
#[derive(Clone)]
pub(super) struct Change{old:Expr,new:Expr,pub requirements:Vec<Stmt>}
fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
impl Change{
    pub fn matches(&self,write:&Expr,column:&str,p:&ProgramDecl)->bool{
        let Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,keys,fields,..}=write else{return false;};
        let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=&self.old else{return false;};let Some(old)=read(base)else{return false;};
        fn stable(e:&Expr,p:&ProgramDecl)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name=="viewer"||p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>stable(value,p),_=>false}}
        old.keys.iter().all(|k|stable(&k.value,p))&&keys.iter().all(|k|stable(&k.value,p))&&name==column&&old.table==*table&&old.keys.len()==keys.len()&&old.keys.iter().zip(keys).all(|(a,b)|a.column==b.column&&crate::print::expression(&a.value)==crate::print::expression(&b.value))&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column&&f.value.as_ref().is_some_and(|v|crate::print::expression(v)==crate::print::expression(&self.new)))
    }
}
// Unknown calls and writes end the pending interval; pure leading helper
// requirements can be recovered only inside the immediately tested branch.
pub(super) fn expression_effectful(e:&Expr)->bool{
    let mut e=e.clone();let mut unknown=false;super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Write{..}|Expr::Call{..}){unknown=true;});unknown
}
pub(super) fn prior_effects(e:&Expr)->bool{
    let mut e=e.clone();let(mut calls,mut writes)=(false,0);
    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|match e{Expr::Write{..}=>writes+=1,Expr::Call{..}=>calls=true,_=>{}});
    calls||writes>1
}
pub(super) fn effectful(s:&Stmt)->bool{match s{
    Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}|Stmt::Require{condition:value,..}=>expression_effectful(value),
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>expression_effectful(condition)||then_body.iter().chain(else_body).any(effectful),
    Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Emit{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}

fn prefix(_catalog:&Catalog,body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Stmt>{
    let mut env=env.clone();let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{if effectful(s){break;}if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&env));}},
        _=>break,
    }}out
}
pub(super) fn after(catalog:&Catalog,condition:&Expr,then_body:&[Stmt],else_body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Change>{
    if expression_effectful(condition){return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=condition else{return Vec::new();};
    let body=match op.as_str(){"!="=>then_body,"="=>else_body,_=>return Vec::new()};
    if body.iter().any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Return{..})||effectful(s)){return Vec::new();}
    let requirements=prefix(catalog,body,env);if requirements.is_empty(){return Vec::new();}
    [(left,right),(right,left)].into_iter().filter(|(old,_)|matches!(old.as_ref(),Expr::Member{..})).map(|(old,new)|Change{old:*old.clone(),new:*new.clone(),requirements:requirements.clone()}).collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(write_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(creation_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(result_advice::notes(bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}
pub fn read_sites(program: &Program) -> Vec<ReadSite> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    stmts_read_sites(&program.body, &mut out);
    out
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ReadSite {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    /// `point`, `scan`, or `walk`.
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub take: u64,
}

fn stmts_read_sites(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_read_sites(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_read_sites(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_read_sites(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                stmts_read_sites(then_body, out);
                stmts_read_sites(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_read_sites(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_read_sites(start, out); expr_read_sites(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_read_sites(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_read_sites(&recurse.seed, out);
                        out.push(ReadSite { site: recurse.site.clone(), table: recurse.table.clone(), index: recurse.index.clone(), kind: "walk", take: recurse.depth as u64 * recurse.fanout.max(1) as u64 });
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_read_sites(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_read_sites(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                out.push(ReadSite { site: current.site.clone(), table: current.table.clone(), index: current.index.clone(), kind: "point", take: 1 });
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_read_sites(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_read_sites(&condition.setup, out); expr_read_sites(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_read_sites(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_read_sites(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_read_sites(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_read_sites(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
-- Food Truck Permits: city health department permits for food trucks.
--
-- Roles: operator (truck owners), inspector (field inspections and the live
-- board), supervisor (environmental health supervisor: suspends permits and
-- approves operators with a past suspension, recording the reason).
-- Role rows are provisioned by the local operator (service:operator), the
-- same pattern as `guide staging`: signup alone grants no role.
use identity password

table roster:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roster[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roster[viewer]

invariant roster.read only .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
invariant roster.insert only viewer = 'service:operator'

-- Trucks belong to exactly one operator. activePermit points at the truck's
-- current permit once one is issued (maintained by approveApplication).
table trucks:
  owner: principal
  name: text 1..80
  licensePlate: text 1..16
  activePermit: permits ?
  unique licensePlate else PLATE_TAKEN
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation registerTruck(name: text 1..80, licensePlate: text 1..16):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return insert trucks { owner: viewer, name, licensePlate }

query myTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  return trucks where .owner = viewer first 50 by .id after c

query allTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return trucks first 50 by .id after c

invariant trucks.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- Each application must include interior photos, the fire extinguisher tag
-- photo and the permit fee: all three columns are required, so an
-- application row cannot exist without them.
table applications:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4
  extinguisherTag: image <=5000000
  feeCents: int 1..100000000
  status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed') default 'applied'
  failedAt: time default now
  lastPassed: bool default false
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable truck, owner, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents, createdAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation submitApplication(truck: trucks, interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4, extinguisherTag: image <=5000000, feeCents: int 1..100000000):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  t = trucks[truck] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require t.owner = viewer else NOT_YOUR_TRUCK
  flag = operatorFlags[viewer]
  require flag = null or flag.suspended = false else OPERATOR_SUSPENDED
  return insert applications { truck, owner: viewer, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents }

query myApplications(c: cursor ?):
  return applications where .owner = viewer first 50 by .createdAt after c

-- The live board: every application as it moves applied -> inspection recorded ->
-- approved / failed. Staff only.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return applications first 50 by .createdAt after c

invariant applications.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant applications.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- v2 renamed the 'inspected' stage to 'inspection recorded'. The column still
-- admits the old value so that every historical row survives adoption and
-- rollback without dormancy; the migrateInspectionRecorded mutation below
-- moves every remaining 'inspected' row to the renamed stage (run after
-- deploy and after every forward), and no workflow mutation writes the old
-- value anymore. Rollback to a release without the renamed value projects
-- 'inspection recorded' back to 'inspected' instead of hiding rows.
down applications inspectedView(status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed')): {status: 'inspected' if status = 'inspection recorded' else status}

-- One-shot data migration for the v2 rename. Supervisor-only. Moves a single
-- remaining 'inspected' application to 'inspection recorded'; callers page
-- the board until none remain.
mutation migrateInspectionRecorded(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspected' else NOT_INSPECTED
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded' }
  return applications[app.id]

-- Inspections are written by inspectors (or supervisors). owner is the truck
-- owner's principal, denormalized so operators read their own reports and
-- staff read everything.
table inspections:
  truck: trucks
  application: applications ?
  permit: permits ?
  inspector: principal
  passed: bool
  notes: text <=2000 ?
  kind: enum('initial', 'reinspection', 'routine')
  inspectedAt: time default now
  owner: principal
  immutable truck, application, permit, inspector, passed, notes, kind, inspectedAt, owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- First inspection of an application: applied -> inspection recorded.
mutation inspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'applied' else NOT_APPLIED
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'initial', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Reinspection of a failed application, only within ten days of the failure.
mutation reinspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'failed' else NOT_FAILED
  require now <= app.failedAt + 10d else REINSPECTION_LATE
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Decision on an inspection-recorded application. Approval issues the permit, valid
-- 365 days; rejection marks it failed and starts the ten-day reinspection
-- window.
mutation approveApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  p = insert permits { truck: app.truck, owner: app.owner, application: app.id, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + 365d, status: 'active', flagged: false, failedAt: now }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'approved' }
  update trucks[app.truck] { activePermit: p.id }
  return p

mutation rejectApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require not app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'failed', failedAt: now }
  return applications[app.id]

query myInspections(c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .owner = viewer first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query applicationInspections(application: applications, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .application = application first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query permitInspections(permit: permits, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .permit = permit first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

-- Permits are issued on approval and live 365 days. A failed routine
-- inspection flags the permit; a passing reinspection within ten days
-- clears the flag, otherwise the daily sweep suspends it.
table permits:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  application: applications
  issuedAt: time default now
  expiresAt: time
  failedAt: time default now
  flagged: bool default false
  status: enum('active', 'suspended', 'expired') default 'active'
  immutable truck, owner, application, issuedAt, expiresAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- Routine inspection (or reinspection) of an active permit.
mutation inspectPermit(permit: permits, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  i = insert inspections { truck: p.truck, permit: p.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection' if p.flagged else 'routine', owner: p.owner }
  update permits[p.id] { flagged: not passed, failedAt: now }
  return i

query myPermits(c: cursor ?):
  return permits where .owner = viewer first 50 by .issuedAt after c

  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return permits first 50 by .issuedAt after c

invariant permits.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- Suspensions are written by the supervisor (recording the reason) or by
-- the daily sweep when the ten-day reinspection window lapses (reason
-- recorded, suspendedBy null for the automatic case).
table suspensions:
  permit: permits
  owner: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  suspendedBy: principal ?
  suspendedAt: time default now
  immutable permit, owner, reason, suspendedBy, suspendedAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation suspendPermit(permit: permits, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
  upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
  return insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason, suspendedBy: viewer }

query mySuspensions(c: cursor ?):
  return suspensions where .owner = viewer first 50 by .suspendedAt after c

invariant suspensions.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- An operator whose permit was suspended cannot apply again until the
-- supervisor approves them, recording the reason.
table operatorFlags:
  operator: principal
  suspended: bool default true
  unique operator
  immutable operator
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

table approvals:
  operator: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  approvedBy: principal
  approvedAt: time default now
  immutable operator, reason, approvedBy, approvedAt
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation approveOperator(operator: principal, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  flag = operatorFlags[operator] ! else OPERATOR_UNKNOWN
  require flag.suspended else OPERATOR_NOT_SUSPENDED
  update operatorFlags[operator] { suspended: false }
  return insert approvals { operator, reason, approvedBy: viewer }

query myFlag():
  return operatorFlags[viewer]

query myApprovals(c: cursor ?):
  return approvals where .operator = viewer first 50 by .approvedAt after c

query flaggedOperators(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  return operatorFlags where .suspended = true first 50 by .id after c

-- Daily sweep: suspend active flagged permits whose reinspection window
-- lapsed, and expire permits past their 365-day life.
job sweepPermits(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
  for p in permits:
    if p.status = 'active' and p.flagged and p.failedAt < periodEnd - 10d:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
      insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason: 'No passing reinspection within ten days of the failed inspection.', suspendedAt: periodEnd }
      upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
    if p.status = 'active' and p.expiresAt <= periodEnd:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'expired', flagged: false }

// Identify direct argument supplies to declared column-write intent. This only
// prioritizes proposals; actual interpreter admission still decides every case.
pub(super) fn write_arguments(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
    fn walk(v:&serde_json::Value,f:&mut impl FnMut(&serde_json::Value)){
        f(v);match v{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){walk(x,f)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{walk(x,f)},_=>{}}
    }
    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();
    walk(&json,&mut |v|for tag in ["Const","Let"]{if let Some(b)=v.get(tag)&&let(Some(n),Some(e))=(b.get("name").and_then(|v|v.as_str()),b.get("value")){bindings.insert(n.to_string(),e.clone());}});
    fn resolve<'a>(v:&'a serde_json::Value,bindings:&'a BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->&'a serde_json::Value{
        if depth>32{return v;}if let Some(n)=v.get("Variable").and_then(|v|v.as_str())&&let Some(e)=bindings.get(n){resolve(e,bindings,depth+1)}else{v}
    }
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    walk(&json,&mut |v|for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
        let Some(w)=v.get(tag)else{continue;};let table=w.get("table").or_else(||w.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(|v|v.as_str());
        let Some(payload)=w.get("row").or_else(||w.get("patch"))else{continue;};let payload=resolve(payload,&bindings,0);
        let Some(fields)=payload.get("Object").and_then(|v|v.as_object())else{continue;};
        for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|Some(i.table.as_str())==table&&i.column.is_some()&&!matches!(i.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")&&i.operation.as_ref().is_none_or(|op|op==&p.name)){
            if let Some(value)=fields.get(inv.column.as_ref().unwrap())&&let Some(arg)=resolve(value,&bindings,0).get("Variable").and_then(|v|v.as_str())&&p.args.contains_key(arg){out.insert(arg.into());}
        }
    });out
}
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}

        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }
    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)) if !matches!(inner.as_ref(),ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    for case in cases{
        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
        }}
        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    }prioritized
}
